      Words

      The product's own terms, in plain words, with the internal names behind them and the words they are easily confused with. Each state in Part 3 shows its member-facing word first. 126 of those are working names proposed from the definitions, marked working name, for the design to settle.

      TermPlain meaningInternal namesEasily confused with
      GroupThe people who run one copy of CivicOS together; it can be one person. instance, group slug, group-instance
      Copy (of CivicOS)The group's own installation, in the group's own hosting account. instance, bio-plane, Workera copy of a document (a capture)
      FounderThe person who claimed the copy first; the root of trust. rootOfTrust, claim, bootstrap credential
      MemberA person enrolled in the group with a handle and password. member:<id>, handle, cover
      AdministratorA member who manages membership and group settings, but has no power inside a project they have not joined. administer, adminendorse, adminremove
      CapabilityA permission a member holds, such as contribute; without it the act is not shown. capabilities, contribute, create_projects, membercaps
      InvitationA one-time link that lets a new person enrol; once used it is gone. invite, invitelook, enroll
      ProjectA shared workspace where some members build a case together; hidden unless made discoverable. project, projectinvite, projectjoin, visibilitya question: a project holds questions, it is not one
      OwnerA project member who can invite, remove and publish for that project. project_owner, projectowneradd, ownerMath
      Joined participantA member who has joined a project and may act inside it. joined, project_participant, roster
      DocumentAnything the group holds as evidence: a captured page or file, or a member's own written observation. information, bundle, information bundle
      CaptureFetching a public document and keeping its exact bytes, with where and when they came from. acquire, capture, captureShacapture grade (how well the capture is established)
      Release (vouch for)A named member states a document appears to be what it claims; not that it is true. release, verified, promotion R31, ratification R20-R27 (op=release)publish: releasing keeps it inside the group
      RetireTake a document out of use, with a reason; it is kept forever but can no longer be cited, and one a live citation rests on cannot be retired. ratification R28-R33; K830retire, retired, ratification R28-R33 (op=retire)
      ProvenanceThe record of where a document came from and how it reached the group. provenance, route, provenancechain
      Grade (A-D)A letter for how easily someone else could check something for themselves, never whether it is true. Capture, connection, testimony and subject match share one A-D meaning, each with its own colour and icon, and are never added up. Grade A is a ceiling, not a minimum (the venue sets the standard); a doorbell document has no fetched letter, only the member's authored letter, stated as authored. DEC-82; System Design §4; Intake Doctrine §3; provenance R51capture grade, connection grade, testimony gradestrength, which is the weakest grade along a chain
      PassageA marked part of a document (a page region or text span) that can be cited on its own. content, contentmint, mint
      Reading (of a document)What the system extracted from a document's bytes, with how reliable that extraction is. extraction, tier, pdfstructurereading of a question's evidence (a basis version), which the old UI also calls a reading
      TranscriptionA member typing what part of a document says; it counts more once a different member confirms it. transcribe, transcriptionattest
      SubjectA person, office, place, law or thing the record knows by name and aliases. entity, entitycreate, resolveSubject (of a plan) / matter: one matter an action plan addresses (which word members see is Bob's to decide)
      ConnectionA link between documents through a shared subject, found by the machine or chosen by a member. connection, connectionchoose, connect
      ThemeA member's own idea with a test that material passes or fails; it gathers material but never proves anything. theme, themedeclare, themeplace
      Flow (progression)How something is supposed to go, as ordered stages, so a missing or late stage shows up. progression, progressiondefine, thread, discharge
      LeadA member's tip or suspicion and where to look; never evidence itself. lead, leadshare, leadlook
      Firsthand observationSomething a member saw or heard, recorded in their own words as a document. testify, firsthand-observationobservation (a look: whether anyone looked and what they found)
      LookA record of whether anyone looked for something and what they found: 'Nobody looked', looked and absent, could not tell, present. 'Nobody looked' has its own look and fixed sentence, distinct from Undetermined ('could not tell', DEC-86). observation-log R1-R3; DEC-86observation, NEVER_LOOKED, LOOKED_ABSENT, LOOKED_INDETERMINATE, PRESENT, frontierfirsthand observation
      QuestionSomething the group is trying to answer, and the evidence it rests on. inquiry, open, surfaced, focus, problem
      FindingA question the group has answered, with what would prove the answer wrong. Case Making §Naming; K356inquiry (concluded), conclude, falsifier'Noticed': the queue class for something the system noticed that nobody has judged (K356)
      LegOne piece of evidence cited for or against a question. leg, cite, citation, basis
      Cite / severAdd a piece of evidence to a question, or withdraw it with a reason; withdrawn evidence stays listed. cite, sever, reinstate
      Reading of the evidence (basis version)One way of arranging a question's evidence; members accept or turn it down, and a project picks one to stand on. basis-version, versionaccept, versioncurrent, CURRENTreading of a document (extraction)
      StrengthHow well a question is supported: the weakest capture and weakest connection, shown as a pair, never one score. strength R1-R10; DEC-44; DEC-82strength pair, inquirystrength, graded / unrated / undeterminedgrade: strength is read off the weakest grade
      BarThe strength a project (or the group by default) requires before it will publish. strength R14-R16; DEC-82required_strength, strengthbar
      UndeterminedWhat we do not know, and why: one mark with a line that always says 'because…', shown the same way everywhere, never as an error or a zero (DEC-86). DEC-86; Interaction Constructs §Uundetermined, UNDETERMINEDits four neighbours, each with its own look and its own fixed sentence, never shared (DEC-86): withheld (it exists, you may not see it), unrated (nothing graded yet), nobody looked, refused (the system declined an act)
      Lens (declared bias)The group's or project's stated viewpoint, adopted openly so readers can weigh it. bias, bias-set, biasadopt, manifest
      Bias debt / hunch debtBias debt: the lens changed since work was done, disclosed. Hunch debt: a connection graded ahead of its evidence, must be cleared before publishing. biasdebt, biasdebtresolve, huncheach other: bias debt never blocks, hunch debt does
      CaseWhat a project publishes: a set of findings and evidence, signed, that anyone can check. case, publish, case document, editiona question or finding (before DEC-72 a case was a phase of a question)

      PublishPut a case across the fence for anyone to read, through the publication ceremony; it cannot be taken back, only corrected by a new edition. Publication §5; DEC-80publish, irreversible, edition

      StandardA rule, law or commitment the group holds in its own words, with its source and period. standard, standarddeclare, standardadopt

      Display primitives

      The few ways the product shows certainty, absence, weight and restriction. Each should look the same wherever it appears.

      The measures map: letters grade evidence, bars show progress, weights mark acts

      one frame for every scale a member meets. One visual form per scale, used everywhere and for nothing else; colour marks the scale, never the value; every mark opens to its plain question; an answer is only as strong as the weakest thing it depends on; a grade tells how easily someone else could check it, never whether it is true

      Where: every grade, bar and act button

      DEC-82; Interaction Constructs §M; docs/development/ux-substrate/measures-map.html (approved)

      Undetermined (one component, always 'because…')

      what we do not know and why: one component with a fixed mark, identical everywhere, and a mandatory 'because…' line; first-class, never an error, never a zero, written as a named term. A newcomer meets it first, beside strength against the bar

      Where: legs, strength axes, capture route, risk tier, standard source and in-force, consequences, home sets, deliverer, profiles, a stage the record cannot read

      DEC-86; Interaction Constructs §U, §M; System Design §1

      Its four neighbours: Withheld, Unrated, Nobody looked, Refused

      each visibly different from Undetermined and from each other, each with one fixed sentence pattern of its own, never shared: Withheld (exists, not yours to see; incompleteness only, never what or how many); Unrated (nothing graded yet: rests on nothing); Nobody looked (no one searched there; not the same as looked and absent); Refused (the system declined an act, naming its reason). None is dressed as an error

      Where: wherever Undetermined can appear

      DEC-86; Interaction Constructs §U; DEC-36; strength R3; observation-log R1

      Evidence letters A-D (one shared scale)

      every evidence scale (capture, connection, testimony, subject match) shares one A-D meaning: A is easiest for someone else to check, D rests on a person's word. Each scale has its own colour and descriptive icon (document, link, speech, person) and the same badge shape; scales are never added up and never take disjoint letters

      Where: captures, subject matches, connections, legs, flow instances

      DEC-82; Interaction Constructs §M; System Design §4; entities R9; provenance R24-R27

      Strength pair and the bar

      the weakest capture and weakest connection, side by side, testimony beside; graded, 'Unrated' or 'Undetermined' with its reason; never one score. In the project workspace each question shows its two badges and one phrase against the bar ('Meets the bar', 'Short on connection', 'Short on capture', 'Unrated'); hover names the weakest document or link and how to raise it; 'Unrated' has its own treatment and fixed sentence, distinct from Undetermined (DEC-86)

      Where: questions, versions, the project workspace, published cases, determinations

      strength R1-R10; publication R11; DEC-44; DEC-82 (question 5); DEC-86

      Progress bars (stage, readiness, source identity)

      each ladder has its own colour family and texture (solid, striped, dotted); dashed means 'not yet evaluated'; every step is named, reached or not. A project's stage stacks one bar per stage reached; hover, focus or tap discloses more and a click on a reached step shows how it was reached; 'closed' shows its reason and never reads as a finished stack. Readiness shows each rung by its own evidence; a published case reads Distributed, never Draft

      Where: project home, the project's cases, a source's identity

      DEC-79; DEC-82; K364; DEC-78 item 5; State Rules §4.3; publication R44-R49

      Weight marks on acts (rung and friction)

      every act's weight: reversible < reasoned < terminal < attested < irreversible. every act's button carries its rung's name and weight pips, so weight is read before the click. Friction follows the rung: reversible inline; reasoned a reason field in place; terminal and attested a full dialog stating what ends or cannot be silently undone, and who signs; irreversible only through the ceremony. An act whose effect cannot be taken back outside the record opens the full dialog whatever its rung. An act with no rung is treated as reasoned

      Where: every act option

      affordances R2-R3, R27; Interaction Constructs §revised set, §F; DEC-87; DEC-88

      Set weight

      refuse (all or nothing), report (proceed, say what moved), per-item (each applied or kept with reason), single

      Where: bulk acts on selections and queue sets

      affordances R1; record-core R50-R52

      Look states: 'Nobody looked', looked and absent, could not tell, partial, present

      which absence is true, at which of four levels; 'Nobody looked' is written as a named term, as Undetermined is; 'nobody looked' has its own treatment and fixed sentence, never Undetermined's (DEC-86)

      Where: frontier, leads, run logs, searched section of a case

      observation-log R1-R3; skills R19; case-authoring R17; DEC-82; DEC-86

      Content-axis states

      indexed full / partial / none, not extracted, undetermined

      Where: documents and passages

      observation-log R1, R12

      'Machine work' label

      the one treatment shared by every machine output: anything a machine proposed or recommended, never evidence until a member acts; with attribution ('the assistant did this, at <member>'s request'). A recommendation is labelled, shown first with its reason, and accepted in one attributed act the record remembers

      Where: suggested versions, proposed readings, standards, comparisons, laws, tiers, filings, contradiction labels and recommendations, the assistant panel

      AI Roles §3 rule 3; DEC-77 item 3; DEC-90; filings R5; standards R9

      Origin mark (partly ruled)

      who made this, in the same family as 'Machine work': Ours; Another group's (a published edition, signature verified); Accepted by our group (a reasoned act, with its reason); Not yet evaluated; Flagged (only a member's recorded evaluation naming specific issues, never a machine's). Ambient; hover shows the fact behind it; never composed with grades or strength into a trust score. 'Meets standards' waits for an evaluator of incoming work; more is Bob's to decide later

      Where: information from other groups, cited work, documents

      DEC-92; Roadmap §11 (revised); inquiry R7

      Tension mark

      on any side of an unresolved record contradiction: in tension, softened (explained, not yet shown), stale (corrected, with its reason) or qualified (dissolved, with its qualifier); a world conflict shows as a quiet lead. Opens the 'how do these differ?' clarifier; published as an attributed sentence. Also held irreconcilable, and unseen conflict on a member's own side when the other side is in a record they cannot see

      Where: legs, versions, determinations, cases, subjects, changed captures, two projects' conclusions

      DEC-76; DEC-77 items 1-2; DEC-84 (3), (7); CONTRADICTION-PRESENT-RESOLVE-DESIGN.md §5; contradiction R27; DEC-85

      Co-attested / 'self-attested only'

      a published case states each document's capture grade and whether it is co-attested (a trusted timestamp and a third-party co-archive); a document whose co-attestation failed is marked 'self-attested only', with the owner's acknowledgement; a late attestation says it proves the bytes existed by its date, not at capture

      Where: documents, the ceremony, published cases

      DEC-81 items 1, 3; Intake Doctrine §3; capture R68

      Agent-surfaced marker

      this question was raised by the assistant

      Where: wherever a question is listed or shown

      System Design §3 row 12 (D-82); ai-runs R27

      Withheld / out of view

      something exists you may not see: stated as incompleteness, never identity or count; its own treatment and one fixed sentence, never Undetermined's (DEC-86)

      Where: queue homes, strength members, re-evaluation dependents, observation rows

      queue R7 (DEC-36); strength R6 (withheld whole, built T12, K360); reevaluation R3; observation-log R13; DEC-86

      Existence-only project

      you see the name of a discoverable project, nothing inside

      Where: project directory, acts naming a project

      membership R44, R48, R77

      Stale / newer version notice

      a passage was cited under an earlier reading or a newer capture exists (affected / undetermined); a notice, never a silent change

      Where: legs, cites, claims, the queue

      content R22; reevaluation R14; queue R9, R12 (built T12, N172)

      basis_changed flag

      something a determination, consequence or packet rests on moved; nothing recomputed

      Where: layer 9 objects

      conformance R10; consequences R8; filings R12

      Notable departure

      a project departs from a group aspiration, with reason

      Where: wherever project aspirations are read

      intent R10

      Not a publication marking

      review copy is unsigned and never leaves

      Where: review copy

      review R11

      Counsel marking and [UNFILLED: name]

      not legal advice, not for filing; blank the record could not fill

      Where: filings

      filings R3, R10

      Truncated / bound

      a list was cut at a stated bound

      Where: every list

      membership R48; queue R6; intent Bounds

      Refused: the standard sentence, then what to do

      every refusal names its reason in plain words before anything runs; where refusals of one condition share a code, the member reads the shared sentence first and then the act's own next step or alternative, as part of what they read; 'Refused' has its own treatment and fixed sentence, never Undetermined's (DEC-86)

      Where: every act

      membership Provides, R84; DEC-83; affordances Purpose (pre-flight, DEC-8); AI Roles §3 rule 10; DEC-86

      Ballot arithmetic

      votes needed, eligible, have, awaiting

      Where: administrator and owner votes

      membership R5-R7, R38-R40

      Mute / snooze (personal) vs dispose (record)

      personal quieting never changes the record; dispositions are attributed; a record contradiction's duty is never muted

      Where: queue

      queue Purpose, R14, R20; DEC-84 (2)

      Support in words (established, short, 'hypothetical: not yet shown')

      A plan subject's support written out, never by colour alone; a plan resting only on hypothetical subjects says so at the top as hunch debt.

      Where: plan page subjects; the Start preview; the Plans list

      PATH.md §2 step 2; action-plans R6; build/plan/action-design/plan-page.html (approved by Bob, K608 (4))

      The plan's checks strip

      Checks in words under the plan's title, each linking to what it is about; informs, never refuses or changes anything.

      Where: plan page

      action-plans R19; PATH.md §1

      Assistant tray marker

      'Suggested by the assistant · for a <kind of work> project · not yet in the plan', each with why; the one machine-work treatment.

      Where: plan page

      PATH.md §2 step 3; build/plan/action-design/plan-page.html (approved by Bob, K608 (4)); DEC-90

      Origin tag on an option

      'adopted from a suggestion' on an option that came from the assistant.

      Where: options list

      build/plan/action-design/plan-page.html (approved by Bob, K608 (4)); action-plans R11

      Regulated date with its basis

      Every date set by law or order shows where it comes from ('ends 20 Nov, basis: the election-contest window (from the profile)'); a date past, or before its phase can start, is a check.

      Where: options, scenarios, actions

      action-plans Terms, R19; build/plan/action-design/plan-page.html (approved by Bob, K608 (4))

      Ours / theirs on a deadline

      A queue item says whose it is: the group's own (a checkpoint, 'obligation · ours') or the counterparty's ('the counterparty's deadline, not ours').

      Where: queue

      PATH.md §3; build/plan/action-design/surfaces.html (design view, not stated as approved)

      Nearing deadline (display, not notification)

      As a deadline nears, an item's position, colour or wording may change; nothing is sent.

      Where: queue; action page

      DEC-94 (2); action-clocks R8

      'Rests on an unestablished premise:' disclosure

      First on the face of an overridden action and of every filing, counsel packet and communication prepared from it, with reason, author and time.

      Where: action page; filings; exports

      actions R8; filings R24; build/plan/action-design/start-and-send.html (design view, not stated as approved)

      In-band stamp line

      On approved filings, communications and every counsel-packet export: hash, date, author, both thresholds; a draft carries none.

      Where: filings; communications; exports

      filings R22; build/plan/action-design/start-and-send.html (design view, not stated as approved)

      Exhibit grade beside the venue's standard

      Each exhibit's letter grade and co-attestation, the venue's standard beside it where the profile states it, a contestable grade flagged; 'undetermined' when the profile states none.

      Where: filings; counsel packet

      filings R25; jurisdictions R39

      Addressee by role and organisation

      Who an action is addressed to, always as a role with its body or organisation, or a described audience; never a person's name alone.

      Where: options; actions; Start preview

      actions R9; PATH.md §3

      Liveness mark

      A plan subject superseded (naming its successor), closed or removed, derived when read.

      Where: plan page

      action-plans R8

      Pressure mark

      A received reply marked legal, retaliation, discrediting or other, listed apart, 'kept as evidence'.

      Where: action page; matter page

      actions R48; build/plan/action-design/matter-page.html (design view, not stated as approved)

      3Intake and provenance

      Document (information item) and its capturesBuilt, with gapsA public document the group has brought in, held as exact captured bytes with a record of where and when each version was fetched.

      Module capture, provenance, capture-sources, promotion capture Purpose; provenance Purpose, R22-R32; promotion R16, R31; State Rules §4.1; Intake Doctrine §3; DEC-78 item 2; DEC-81; capture R16, R20, R68, R69; provenance R19, R42, R51

      What a member sees: address and retrieval locator, capture grade (B direct, C archive, authored when unrecorded), versions at the address, origin system, source status, its source, as it stood when received: the person who handed it over (named or unnamed) or the unnamed knocker, apart from the member who captured it (DEC-78 item 2), capture.actor, the member of a member session (capture R16), attestation attempts at capture: every acquisition requests a trusted timestamp and a co-archive and records each attempt (capture R20), late attestations: 'proves the bytes existed by <at>, not at capture', with whether the replay matched (capture R68), the capturing member's signed accounts, read only by those who may see the capture's bundle (capture R69), from the doorbell: route doorbell, 'received, not fetched', no fetched grade letter, its chain the knock's receipt (provenance R19, R42, R51; K538, K581)

      Shown as: capture grade letter, version chain, 'held in parts', UNDETERMINED for unrecorded route, capture grade on the one shared A-D scale, with the capture icon; a grade says how easily someone else could check it, never whether it is true (DEC-82), co-attested (a trusted timestamp and a third-party co-archive) or, once a case discloses it, 'self-attested only' (DEC-81), origin mark: Ours, Another group's, Accepted by our group, Not yet evaluated, Flagged (DEC-92, partly ruled), Grade A is a ceiling, not a minimum: the venue sets the standard of evidence (Intake Doctrine §3), a doorbell document carries the member's authored letter under the ceiling, stated as authored (provenance R51)

      4. Source unchanged / changed / gone working namesource status: unchanged / modified / removedwhat monitoring saw at the address; recorded beside the state

      Contradiction candidateBuilt, with gapsTwo statements that seem to conflict. A contradiction is conditional: two statements conflict only if they cannot both hold in the same respect (subject, time or occasion, scope, meaning, observer or method). The machine pairs and labels candidates; a member resolves them.

      Module contradiction contradiction R1-R57, C-60.2-C-60.3, C-93.8-C-93.39; inquiry R46-R48; affordances R2, R3, R7, R8, R14; K488, K491, K516, K544; build/plan/archive/T15.md, T16.md, T17.md; DEC-76, DEC-77, DEC-84, DEC-85; docs/development/CONTRADICTION-PRESENT-RESOLVE-DESIGN.md (reviewed K447)

      What a member sees: key (K1-K5), both sides, each with where it lives, label: world / record / precision / unrelated / undetermined, weight, derived from label and key and never stored: not_shown, lead, duty, plurality (contradiction R24), run, the respects the machine recommends may differ, labelled machine work; never which side is wrong nor a genuine kind (DEC-84 (5); contradiction R37), the acceptance basis of each clarify or resolve: accepted (naming a standing recommendation) or unaided (DEC-77 item 3; contradiction R30), the record's facts per coordinate (time or occasion, scope, meaning, observer or method, subject), labelled the record's (contradiction R28), reach: only projects the viewer sees in full; a hidden project is neither named nor counted (contradiction R55; K544), a candidate list names an empty level none_judged or none_shown, and offers a default_question for take-up (contradiction R25)

      Shown as: a tension mark inline, wherever the two points already sit together, opening the 'how do these differ?' clarifier; the dedicated screen only as the escalation (DEC-77 item 1), marks every surface reads (contradiction R27): in_tension, softened, stale (naming the member, instant and act), qualified, held_irreconcilable, lead, plurality, unseen_conflict, six forms, one per purpose: compare view, timeline, assumptions checklist, competing-hypotheses matrix, Criteria-Condition-Cause-Effect-Recommendation for an obligation-against-act finding, in-place tension marker (DEC-77 item 2), published: an unresolved record contradiction disclosed on the case in one of four sentences; publication discloses, never blocks (DEC-76 item 4; DEC-84 (13); case-authoring R31-R33), a stale side moves nothing by itself: whoever relies on it gets a re-evaluation notice (cause corrected), and the mark shows wherever the side does (DEC-84 (7); reevaluation R27), a conflict whose other side the viewer cannot see is told only on their own side, as a conflict notice (DEC-85; see the conflict notice), the acceptance rate of recommendations and the dismissal measure, in-process (contradiction R39, R40), reviewed at 95% or more over 30 or more offered (provisional; K447 point 15)

      Lifecycle

      1. Machine suggestion, not judged working nameopen (weight derived at every read)no member has acted on it yet. Its weight comes from its label and key at every read and is never stored: not_shown (a precision or unrelated label: counted, never shown), lead, duty or plurality
      2. Leadlead (world, or undetermined)a conflict about the world: taken up or dismissed with a reason; never a duty

      4. Different conclusions, each attributed working nameplurality (weight, K5)two projects' different conclusions, each attributed; a clarify with no_difference makes it a duty on both projects (R34, not yet met). Not shown to members until the recommender is measured (K488)
      5. Explained, not yet shownexplained, not yet shown (explained_not_shown)a member said how the two differ without evidence on the screen: the mark softens and does not clear
      6. Being looked into working nametaken up (taken_up)opened as one contradiction inquiry, the dedicated screen; one inquiry per candidate
      7. Resolved: dissolved / corrected / genuine working nameresolved (with its kind)its kind recorded (inquiry R46): dissolved (a respect differed; both stay, each qualified); corrected: misquote, transcription_or_reading_error, superseded_version or corrected (the wrong side goes stale with its reason, never deleted); genuine: double_speak_or_reversal (a finding), obligation_against_act (routed into a determination), conflict_of_norms, or irreconcilable (held irreconcilable)

      8Publication

      Case and edition (publication)Built, with gapsWhat a project publishes: one or more concluded findings the group stands behind, signed and permanent, so anyone can verify it; corrections are new editions.

      Module case-authoring, ratification, publication publication Purpose, Terms, R1-R22, R50-R52; case-authoring Purpose, R1-R21, R31-R37; ratification R1-R8, R18-R19; affordances R5, R28; Publication §1-§3; build/plan/archive/T15.md, T16.md layer 8; DEC-76 item 4; DEC-80; DEC-81; DEC-84; DEC-85

      What a member sees: project, edition, findings: load-bearing / supporting, pinned, scope, statement and who wrote it, exclusions, searched section, bias acknowledgement, frozen strength per finding, signer and deliverer, for an obligation-against-act finding: Criteria and Condition as facts from the two sides; Cause only when evidenced, else 'cause not established'; Effect from consequences; Recommendation a proposed action, never a policy position (DEC-84 (10)), case document format bio-case-document/5 (/1-/4 read as written): a tension section; a captures: block {capture, member, grade, grade_basis, co_attested, timestamp_at, co_archive, late, self_attested_only, acknowledgement}; a capture_accounts: block, each signed account verifiable from the document alone; a sources: block {capture, stated, basis} with no source id (publication R20; K549, K553), each tension disclosed in one of four sentences: 'In tension, not yet resolved', 'Explained, not yet shown', 'Held irreconcilable by the group', 'Rests on a side in conflict with a record not shown'; a leg with no passage stated unread (case-authoring R31, R33; K499)

      Shown as: irreversible: reached only through the publication ceremony (DEC-80, DEC-87), NOT_PUBLISHED / FINDING_IN_SEVERAL_CASES, strengthUndetermined CASES_DISAGREE (publication R11), deliverer 'undetermined' when unrecorded, after publication, a tension formed later reaches the project's owners as the FINDING tension-after-publication until a later edition discloses it or it resolves (publication R50; queue-producers R6), a conflict whose other side the publisher cannot see: in the ceremony 'A finding in this case rests on something in conflict with a record you cannot see. You can still publish...'; in the case, highlighted, never naming the hidden record (case-authoring R31-R32; publication R10; DEC-85)

      Conformance determinationBuilt, with gapsThe group's recorded judgment that a named government act was compliant, noncompliant or unclear against named standards, resting on published findings.

      Module conformance conformance Purpose, Terms, R1-R12, R18; DEC-76 item 3; DEC-89; conformance R21, R22, C-113.24-C-113.28; K503, K569, K603; action-plans Terms, R1, R5; docs/architecture/BIO_Action_v0_1.md (canon whole, K608 (1)) §4 rule 4; Bob's ruling 5 (K590)

      Action planBuilt, with gapsA project's working notes for deciding what to do about one or more matters, whether only suspected or already determined: the options it could take, which it chose or declined and why, up to three ways of laying the chosen ones out over time, and what each started option became. It holds no costs, budgets, assignees or hours, and it is never published.

      What a member sees: title (at most 200 characters), project, and the project's declared kind of work (work_kinds), shown, gating nothing, subjects in order, each with support and liveness, options with their dispositions; the assistant's suggestions held apart, up to three scenarios, each started option's action and its lifecycle state; each determined subject's escalation stage, the checks (derived when read, informing only), history: every act, oldest first, with who, when and why

      Shown as: the checks strip in words under the title, each check linking to what it is about (action-plans R19; PATH.md §1), hunch debt at the top when the plan rests only on hypothetical subjects (PATH.md §2 step 2), never a cost, budget, assignee, score or priority (action-plans R26; PATH.md §3)

      Plan subject (a matter the plan addresses)BuiltOne matter a plan is about: either an open question the group suspects (an inquiry) or one standard's outcome of a determination the group has recorded (compliant, noncompliant or unclear). Each shows how well it is supported, so the plan never hides that it rests on a hunch.

      Module action-plans action-plans Terms, R1, R3-R5, R8; PATH.md §2 steps 1-2; K710 (an inquiry is the project's when the project cites it; live states open, surfaced, deferred; 'short' derived against the project's bar when read); K711

      What a member sees: kind: inquiry (suspected; inquiry, act?, standards?) or outcome (determined; determination, standard), support (established, short, hypothetical), liveness (derived), its escalation and stage, when determined (read from escalation R22)

      Lifecycle

      1. establishedsupport: establisheddetermined, and the finding meets the project's bar
      2. shortsupport: shortdetermined, but resting on a finding short of the project's bar
      3. hypothetical: not yet shownsupport: hypotheticalsuspected: an open inquiry, not yet shown
      4. Live / Superseded / Closed / Removed working nameliveness: live / superseded / closed / subject_removedderived when read, never stored: a determination superseded (naming its successor), an inquiry closed, or a subject a member removed
      5. Determined since working namedetermined_sincea determination has since been recorded on the act a suspected subject's inquiry is about; shown beside it until a member adds it

      Option (in an action plan)BuiltOne thing the group could do about the plan's subjects: a demand, a filing, a campaign, a briefing, a meeting. It says which subjects it serves, who it would be addressed to, any dates set by law or order (with where they come from), and for a legal option its risk tier. Leaving it unticked means undecided; the group chooses it, declines it with a reason, or later marks it done or blocked; a chosen one can be started, which creates an action.

      Module action-plans action-plans R9-R13, R18, R26, R29; PATH.md §2 steps 4-6, 9; K711; K836; K837

      What a member sees: summary (at most 200 characters), shown collapsed; detail (at most 5,000), category: mitigation, legal, awareness, journalistic, grassroots or other, subjects served (one or more of the plan's), addressee (actions' shape: an office by role and body; a reporter or outlet, an organisation or another civic group by role and organisation; a described audience; never a private individual), regulated dates, each {date, basis}, tier (legal options only: 1, 2, 3 or undetermined, never defaulted), lobbying mark and what it enforces (a standard or a determined subject), origin: added by a member or adopted from a suggestion (the proposal it came from), revisions with reasons; disposition history, the reminders chosen with it (held with the choice until the action exists)

      Shown as: collapsed as summary, category and dates; expanded: detail, addressee, subjects served, origin, history (PATH.md §2 step 5), sortable by category, regulated start or end date, subject or disposition (PATH.md §2 step 5), a legal option's tier written out, 'undetermined' when unstated, never a default (action-plans R9; PATH.md §3), the profile's venues and legal organisations beside a legal option, labelled as facts, never recommendations (PATH.md §2 step 5; filings R21 availableActions; Bob's ruling 2), 'adopted from a suggestion' tag on an option that came from the assistant (build/plan/action-design/plan-page.html (approved by Bob, K608 (4)))

      4. Done working namedonecarried out

      Checkpoint (the group's own intention, judged by a member)BuiltA point in a scenario where the group has said it will stop and judge how things stand, for example '8 weeks in: the city shows earnest commitment'. A missed or unmet checkpoint is the group's own, never a finding about the government.

      • Group and its copy enrols Member 1 to many · a founder plus members; ordinary members only after two administrators membership R12-R14
      Queue item (and task)Built, with gapsOne thing that wants this member's attention, grouped under every case it belongs to: an obligation a person must do, a finding nobody has judged, or a condition about the copy's own machinery.

      Module queue, tasks, queue-producers queue Purpose, Terms, R1-R29, R39-R40, R46; tasks R1-R9; queue-producers R1-R9, R14 (built T18, K728); Interaction Constructs §QUEUE, DEC-10, DEC-16, DEC-36; K356, K565, K566, K606, K607; DEC-76; DEC-85; queue R1, R5, R12, R28; queue-producers R15-R18; K607; K611; K614; K723; K725; K728; K731

      What a member sees: class and kind, home cases (ancestors to depth 6), summary, detail, basis, age, assignee, options (acts with rung and weight), disposition, item kinds: a newer capture affects a reference (adopt or keep), an objective gap, source modified or removed, archive fallback eligible, recheck due (queue R12; queue-producers R2-R3), a record contradiction waits as a duty until resolved, never muted or dismissed; a world one as a lead (DEC-76 item 3; DEC-84 (1)-(2); queue R46), OBLIGATION kinds: contradiction-duty (instead clarify or take up; resolve once taken up), contradiction-duty-unseen, signer-self-registered (to every administrator; instead signerset; produced since T18, queue-producers R14, K728); each obligation's 'instead' names its own door (queue R1, R12; K607), FINDING kinds: contradiction-lead (dismiss or take up), contradiction-plurality (clarify or take up; not set aside), contradiction-plurality-unseen, side-corrected (reevaluationrecord), tension-after-publication (to project owners; instead publish a new edition) (queue R1, R46; queue-producers R4-R7), obligations come from the tasks module: a task on a subject the viewer may not see reads as no task; the feed reads only open and forwarded tasks, and only the viewer's own and unassigned ones (tasks R3, R6; K565, K566; N410's assignees filter, built T18: K723), Action kinds, built T18 (K725, K728, K731): plan-checkpoint-due (OBLIGATION, the group's own; door checkpointrecord), escalation-stage-proposed (OBLIGATION; door escalationadvance, or escalationdecline), action-reminder (OBLIGATION to the member who set it and nobody else; door reminderanswer), action-clock-overdue (CONDITION, raised once per entry, notifying once), deferred: the litigation-hold item for a legal-kind pressure mark, until Bob designs the hold's act (N-A19; DEC-61; kept by T19's final sweep and draft-T20.md)

      Shown as: out_of_view / undetermined home set (R7), 'resolved by X on date', bias debts included (R39; bias R44), snoozed marker (R40), FINDING never reads as an obligation (R16), a conflict with a record the member cannot see is homed only from the member's own side (queue-producers R7; DEC-85), whose it is: the group's own (a checkpoint) or the counterparty's (a deadline) (PATH.md §3), a missed checkpoint never as a finding (action-plans R23), a nearing deadline changes position, colour or wording only (DEC-94 (2))
